style-guide.md — The Manor Visual System (v4.1 + derived)
UTF-8 · 10 SECTIONS · VERBATIM 
One visual system for everything The Manor makes: HTML pages, documents and PDFs, HyperFrames animations, videos and motion graphics.

Source: `homepage-v4.1.html`. Everything marked **(v4.1)** is taken from the live code. Everything marked **(derived)** applies those rules to a format the homepage doesn't cover. Treat derived lines as a starting point, not a decision.

---

## 1. Emotional emphasis

Every piece should feel like this. If it doesn't, it's off-brand, however tidy it looks.

**Core words (locked):**
edgy · squared (no round edges) · bold · cosmopolitan · revolutionary · raw · urban · unapologetic · rebellious · gritty · underground · punchy · provocative · confident · brutalist

**Supporting words, by feel:**

- **Edgy / rebellious:** defiant, renegade, disruptive, maverick, irreverent
- **Raw / gritty:** stripped-back, industrial, unpolished, rough-cut, honest
- **Bold / punchy:** loud, striking, high-contrast, fearless, commanding
- **Urban / underground:** street, warehouse, indie, DIY, grassroots
- **Straightforward:** no-nonsense, blunt, direct, plain-spoken, no-frills

**What it is not:** soft, rounded, pastel, corporate, friendly-startup, glossy, gradient-heavy, "AI slop".

**Quick test:** Would this look at home pasted on a warehouse wall? Any rounded corners? Is there one clear red moment, or is red everywhere?

---

## 2. Colour (v4.1)

- `--ink` `#141312` — main dark ground
- `--ink-0` `#0D0C0B` — deepest black: showreel, production
- `--ink-2` `#1E1C1B` — cards and panels on ink
- `--line` `#3A3634` — hairlines on dark
- `--bone` `#ECE6DC` — main light ground, text on dark
- `--bone-2` `#E7E0D4` — cards on bone
- `--bone-line` `#CFC7BB` — hairlines on bone
- `--red` `#D7261E` — signal red: CTAs, highlights, markers
- `--red-2` `#B81C16` — service boxes, hover on red
- `--red-deep` `#8E1410` — oxblood ground, offset shadows
- `--red-deeper` `#6D100D` — deepest oxblood tiles
- `--ash` `#9A938A` — secondary text on dark
- `--ink-muted` `#4A453F` — secondary text on bone

**Rules (v4.1):**

- Five grounds only: ink, ink-0, bone, red, oxblood.
- Alternate dark and light sections for rhythm.
- Red is the action colour: buttons, highlight blocks, square markers.
- No decorative gradients. The only gradients are dark overlays on photos.

---

## 3. Typography (v4.1)

**Fonts:** Archivo (variable width) + JetBrains Mono, from Google Fonts.

- **Display / H1 / H2 / H3:** Archivo, width 125, weight 900, UPPERCASE, tracking −0.03 to −0.04em, line-height 0.86–1.0
- **Lede / sub-heads:** Archivo, width 112, weight 600–700, sentence case
- **Body:** Archivo, width 100, weight 400–600, 16px, line-height 1.55
- **Eyebrows, labels, metadata:** JetBrains Mono, weight 700, 11–12px, UPPERCASE, tracking 0.1–0.14em

**Sizes (v4.1):**

- Mega H1: `clamp(56px, 8vw, 144px)`
- H2: `clamp(32px, 4.6vw, 64px)`, max 21 characters wide
- Lede: `clamp(19px, 1.6vw, 24px)`, max 42 characters wide

**Signature moves (v4.1):**

- **Red highlight block:** one key phrase in a heading on a solid red block (e.g. "From zero."). One per heading at most.
- **Eyebrow with red square:** mono label preceded by an 8px red square.
- **Short red rule:** 48 × 3px red bar above a sub-heading.

---

## 4. Shape, lines and texture (v4.1)

- **Zero border radius. Everywhere.** No exceptions.
- **Hairlines:** 1px `--line` on dark, 1px `--bone-line` on light.
- **Hatched rails:** 315° diagonal stripe, 1px line every 10px, down both sides of the content column.
- **Dot grid:** 1px dots on a 12px grid (ink), 14px (oxblood), 24px sparse (ink-0).
- **Diagonal hatch fill:** 315°, 10px tile, for empty frames and placeholders.

## 5. Hard offset shadows (v4.1)

No soft blur shadows. Solid offsets only.

- **S1:** 2px bone border + `10px 10px 0 var(--red)` — the single hero media item
- **S2:** 2px bone border + `8px 8px 0 var(--red-deep)` — lead images, showreel frames, portraits (ink border + ink shadow on bone)
- **S3:** tinted panel, no shadow — groups of content
- **S4:** 1px border + `4px 4px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35)` — secondary frames, thumbnails
- **S5:** hover `translate(-6px,-6px)`, shadow grows to 14px — cards and video frames

## 6. Layout (v4.1)

- Max content width 1600px, with 40px hatched rails each side.
- Inner inset 48px. Gap 20px.
- Section padding 128px top and bottom (72px on mobile).
- Section header: eyebrow bar, then H2 (7 parts) beside lede (5 parts) on wide screens.
- Mobile (<640px): rails disappear, inset 20px, gap 12px.

## 7. Components (v4.1)

- **Buttons:** square, Archivo 125 / 900 / uppercase, 14px, padding 16 × 24px. Red, ink, bone, outline. Hover flips to the opposite ground. `→` for on-page links, `↗` for external.
- **Tags:** Archivo 112 / 700, uppercase, 1px border, solid ground.
- **Cards:** solid colour slabs (red, ink, oxblood, dot grid, bone) with a mono tag bar on top.
- **Albers square:** three nested squares (outer, 20% inset, 40% inset) in three brand colours. Decorative mark.
- **Placeholders:** mono label in a bordered box, e.g. "Photo to come". Never fake content.

## 8. Motion (v4.1)

- Colour and hover transitions: **0.15–0.18s ease**. Fast and snappy, no bounce.
- Hover lift: straight diagonal up-left, 6px.
- Marquees: constant linear scroll, pause on hover, 56–60s per loop.
- Sticky bar: slides up in 0.25s.
- Respect reduced motion: everything stops.

---

## 9. Applying the system to other formats (derived)

### HTML pages and tools

- Single self-contained file. Copy the section 2 tokens as CSS variables.
- Add `*{border-radius:0 !important}` and the blue tap-highlight fix.

### Documents and PDFs

- Page ground: bone. Text: ink. Cover and section-break pages: ink or red.
- Headings Archivo 125 / 900 uppercase. Body Archivo 100. Labels and page numbers JetBrains Mono.
- Hairline rules instead of boxes. Red square markers instead of bullets.
- One red highlight block per page at most.
- Tables: red header row with bone mono text, hairline rows.

### HyperFrames / JavaScript animation

- Same tokens as CSS variables. Same fonts.
- Hard, mechanical movement: cuts, slides, straight-line wipes. No overshoot, no bounce, no blur-in.
- Short timings (0.15–0.3s) for UI-style motion. Linear for continuous scrolls and tickers.
- Shapes enter as solid squares and bars. Text slams in on a cut or wipes in behind a red block.
- Backgrounds use hatch and dot-grid textures, not gradients.

### Video and motion graphics

- Lower-thirds: mono label on a solid ink or red slab, square edges, red square marker.
- Titles: Archivo 125 / 900 uppercase, red highlight block on the key phrase.
- Frames and picture-in-picture: hard offset shadow (S1/S2 style), no rounded corners.
- Transitions: hard cuts and straight wipes in red or ink. No dissolves, no glow.
- Grade: contrast up, saturation slightly down (v4.1 hero image: contrast 1.08, saturation 0.92).

---

## 10. Copy voice

Short. Blunt. Plain words. Full stops do the work.
Examples from v4.1: "Learn to make content. From zero." · "No experience. No gear. No audience." · "We shoot it. You post it."
Never invent facts, prices or client names. Use a plain placeholder until confirmed.

<script>
// Carousel arrows for workshop track (#wtrack at <1280px)
document.querySelectorAll('.icon-btn[data-for]').forEach(b => {
  const t = document.getElementById(b.dataset.for);
  if (!t) return;
  b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const c = t.firstElementChild;
    const gap = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(t).columnGap || getComputedStyle(t).gap) || 12;
    t.scrollBy({ left: (c.offsetWidth + gap) * +b.dataset.dir, behavior: 'smooth' });
  });
});

function arrowState(t) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.icon-btn[data-for="' + t.id + '"]').forEach(b => {
    b.disabled = b.dataset.dir < 0 ? t.scrollLeft < 4 : t.scrollLeft + t.clientWidth >= t.scrollWidth - 4;
  });
}

document.querySelectorAll('.track').forEach(t => {
  arrowState(t);
  t.addEventListener('scroll', () => arrowState(t), { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => arrowState(t), { passive: true });
});

// Click-to-play YouTube facade for showreel (#work, §4.4)
document.querySelectorAll('.work-frame[data-video-id]').forEach(frame => {
  const id = (frame.dataset.videoId || '').trim();
  if (id && id !== 'VIDEO_ID') {
    const ph = frame.querySelector('.ph');
    if (ph) ph.remove();
    const img = document.createElement('img');
    img.className = 'work-thumb';
    img.src = 'https://i.ytimg.com/vi/' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '/hqdefault.jpg';
    img.alt = '';
    img.loading = 'lazy';
    frame.prepend(img);
  }
  const playVideo = () => {
    const vid = (frame.dataset.videoId || '').trim();
    if (!vid || vid === 'VIDEO_ID' || frame.querySelector('iframe')) return;
    const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
    iframe.src = 'https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/' + encodeURIComponent(vid) + '?autoplay=1&rel=0&playsinline=1';
    iframe.title = frame.getAttribute('aria-label') || 'Showreel video';
    iframe.allow = 'autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture; fullscreen';
    iframe.allowFullscreen = true;
    frame.replaceChildren(iframe);
  };
  frame.addEventListener('click', playVideo);
  frame.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
      e.preventDefault();
      playVideo();
    }
  });
});

// Lazy-load YouTube testimonial iframes on scroll into view (§4.8)
const ytObserver = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
  entries.forEach(entry => {
    if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
    const iframe = entry.target;
    const vid = (iframe.dataset.videoId || '').trim();
    const rawSrc = iframe.dataset.src || '';
    if (vid && vid !== 'VIDEO_ID') {
      iframe.src = 'https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/' + encodeURIComponent(vid) + '?autoplay=1&mute=1&cc_load_policy=1&playsinline=1&loop=1&playlist=' + encodeURIComponent(vid) + '&rel=0';
      iframe.closest('.testi-video-frame').classList.add('is-loaded');
    } else if (rawSrc && !rawSrc.includes('VIDEO_ID')) {
      iframe.src = rawSrc;
      iframe.closest('.testi-video-frame').classList.add('is-loaded');
    }
    ytObserver.unobserve(iframe);
  });
}, { rootMargin: '120px' });

document.querySelectorAll('iframe.yt-lazy').forEach(el => ytObserver.observe(el));

// Sticky service bar after hero (§4.17)
const hero = document.getElementById('hero-sec'),
      bar = document.getElementById('sticky'),
      end = document.getElementById('end');
let pastHero = false, atEnd = false;
const sync = () => {
  const on = pastHero && !atEnd;
  bar.classList.toggle('on', on);
  bar.setAttribute('aria-hidden', !on);
  bar.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(a => { a.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1; });
};
new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
  pastHero = !e.isIntersecting && e.boundingClientRect.top < 0;
  sync();
}).observe(hero);
new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
  atEnd = e.isIntersecting;
  sync();
}).observe(end);
</script>
</body>
</html>

  <!-- ============================================================
       01 · EMOTIONAL EMPHASIS (#emphasis)

  <section class="sec-Band ground-ink" id="emphasis">
    <div class="frame">
      <div class="rail rail-l" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="col sec-pad">
        <div class="sec-head">
          <div class="sec-head-top">
            <span class="eyebrow">01 / Emotional Emphasis</span>
            <span class="coord">BRAND POSTURE · 15 CORE WORDS LOCKED · 5 FEEL CLUSTERS</span>
          </div>
          <h2 class="h2">Every piece should <em>feel like this.</em></h2>
          <p class="lede" style="color:var(--ash);">
            If it doesn't, it's off-brand, however tidy it looks.
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- 15 Locked Core Words -->
        <div class="coord" style="margin-bottom:12px;">CORE WORDS (LOCKED — ALL 15):</div>
        <div class="core-words-cloud">
          <span class="core-word highlight">edgy</span>
          <span class="core-word">squared (no round edges)</span>
          <span class="core-word">bold</span>
          <span class="core-word">cosmopolitan</span>
          <span class="core-word">revolutionary</span>
          <span class="core-word">raw</span>
          <span class="core-word">urban</span>
          <span class="core-word highlight">unapologetic</span>
          <span class="core-word">rebellious</span>
          <span class="core-word">gritty</span>
          <span class="core-word">underground</span>
          <span class="core-word">punchy</span>
          <span class="core-word">provocative</span>
          <span class="core-word">confident</span>
          <span class="core-word highlight">brutalist</span>
        </div>

        <!-- Supporting Words by Feel (5 columns) -->
        <div class="coord" style="margin-bottom:14px;">SUPPORTING WORDS, BY FEEL:</div>
        <div class="feel-grid">
          <div class="feel-col">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4">Edgy / rebellious</div>
            <ul class="feel-list">
              <li>defiant</li>
              <li>renegade</li>
              <li>disruptive</li>
              <li>maverick</li>
              <li>irreverent</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="feel-col">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4">Raw / gritty</div>
            <ul class="feel-list">
              <li>stripped-back</li>
              <li>industrial</li>
              <li>unpolished</li>
              <li>rough-cut</li>
              <li>honest</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="feel-col">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4">Bold / punchy</div>
            <ul class="feel-list">
              <li>loud</li>
              <li>striking</li>
              <li>high-contrast</li>
              <li>fearless</li>
              <li>commanding</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="feel-col">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4">Urban / underground</div>
            <ul class="feel-list">
              <li>street</li>
              <li>warehouse</li>
              <li>indie</li>
              <li>DIY</li>
              <li>grassroots</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div class="feel-col">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4">Straightforward</div>
            <ul class="feel-list">
              <li>no-nonsense</li>
              <li>blunt</li>
              <li>direct</li>
              <li>plain-spoken</li>
              <li>no-frills</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- What it is not + Quick Test -->
        <div class="not-vs-quick">
          <div class="not-panel">
            <span class="eyebrow" style="margin-bottom:14px;">What It Is Not (Forbidden)</span>
            <div class="h3" style="margin-bottom:10px;">Never ship these traits.</div>
            <p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--ash);">
              Any trace of startup gloss, rounded corners, or decorative colour blends breaks the system immediately.
            </p>
            <div class="not-tags">
              <span class="not-tag">soft</span>
              <span class="not-tag">rounded</span>
              <span class="not-tag">pastel</span>
              <span class="not-tag">corporate</span>
              <span class="not-tag">friendly-startup</span>
              <span class="not-tag">glossy</span>
              <span class="not-tag">gradient-heavy</span>
              <span class="not-tag">"AI slop"</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="quick-test-panel">
            <div>
              <span class="eyebrow" style="margin-bottom:12px;">The 3-Point Quick Test</span>
              <div class="h3">Run every layout through three blunt questions before shipping.</div>
            </div>
            <div class="qt-grid">
              <div class="qt-item">
                <div class="coord" style="color:var(--red);margin-bottom:8px;">CHECK 01 // POSTURE</div>
                <div class="h4" style="font-size:16px;">Would this look at home pasted on a warehouse wall?</div>
              </div>
              <div class="qt-item">
                <div class="coord" style="color:var(--red);margin-bottom:8px;">CHECK 02 // GEOMETRY</div>
                <div class="h4" style="font-size:16px;">Any rounded corners? (Must be zero everywhere.)</div>
              </div>
              <div class="qt-item">
                <div class="coord" style="color:var(--red);margin-bottom:8px;">CHECK 03 // DISCIPLINE</div>
                <div class="h4" style="font-size:16px;">Is there one clear red moment, or is red everywhere?</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="rail rail-r" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- ============================================================
       02 · COLOUR (v4.1) (#colour)
  ============================================================ -->
  <section class="sec-Band ground-bone" id="colour">
    <div class="frame">
      <div class="rail rail-l" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="col sec-pad">
        <div class="sec-head">
          <div class="sec-head-top">
            <span class="eyebrow">02 / Colour (v4.1)</span>
            <span class="coord">13 LOCKED CSS TOKENS · 5 ALLOWED GROUNDS · CLICK ANY SWATCH TO COPY HEX</span>
          </div>
          <h2 class="h2">Ink, bone &amp; <em>signal red.</em></h2>
          <p class="lede" style="color:var(--ink-muted);">
            Five grounds only: ink, ink-0, bone, red, oxblood. Alternate dark and light sections for rhythm. Red is the action colour. No decorative gradients.
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- All 13 Colour Tokens Swatch Grid -->
        <div class="coord" style="margin-bottom:14px;">ALL 13 V4.1 COLOUR TOKENS (CLICK CARD TO COPY HEX):</div>
        <div class="swatch-grid">
          <div class="swatch-card" data-copy="#141312">
            <div class="swatch-color" style="background:#141312;color:#ECE6DC;">
              <span class="tag tag-ink">GROUND 01</span>
              <span style="font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace;font-size:11px;">COPY</span>
            </div>
            <div class="swatch-body">
              <div class="swatch-var">--ink</div>

            <p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-muted);">Alternate dark and light sections down the page so every boundary cuts sharply.</p>
          </div>
          <div style="background:var(--bone-2);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:20px;">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4" style="margin-bottom:6px;">03. Red Is Action</div>
            <p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-muted);">Reserve signal red for buttons, one highlight block per heading, and 8px square markers.</p>
          </div>
          <div style="background:var(--bone-2);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:20px;">
            <span class="red-rule" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <div class="h4" style="margin-bottom:6px;">04. Zero Gradients</div>
            <p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-muted);">No decorative gradients. The only permitted gradients are dark legibility overlays on photos.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

  <section class="sec-Band ground-ink" id="shadows">

          <p class="lede" style="color:var(--ash);">
            Zero blur radius on every shadow. Hierarchy is controlled strictly by border thickness, offset distance (10px, 8px, 4px, 14px), and shadow slab colour.
          </p>

  <section class="sec-Band ground-bone" id="comp-data">
    <div class="frame">
      <div class="rail rail-l" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="col sec-pad">
        <div class="split" style="margin-bottom:80px;">
          <div>
            <span class="eyebrow" style="margin-bottom:16px;">07.3 / 50:50 Split + Red Square Check List</span>
            <h3 class="h2" style="margin-bottom:16px;">Red squares <em>replace bullets.</em></h3>
            <p style="color:var(--ink-muted);margin-bottom:16px;">
              Never use default browser round disc bullets. Every list item sits between 1px hairlines and starts with a solid <code style="color:var(--ink);">8px</code> or <code style="color:var(--ink);">10px</code> <code style="color:var(--ink);">--red</code> square.
            </p>
            <ul class="checks" style="border-color:var(--bone-line);">
              <li style="border-color:var(--bone-line);">Dedicated industrial desk in Manor House N4</li>
              <li style="border-color:var(--bone-line);">10Gbps symmetrical fibre for raw footage uploads</li>
              <li style="border-color:var(--bone-line);">Member credits toward studio and booth hire</li>
              <li style="border-color:var(--bone-line);">No corporate beanbags. No startup ping-pong.</li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span class="eyebrow" style="margin-bottom:16px;">07.4 / Accordion Component (&lt;details class="faq-item"&gt;)</span>
            <div class="faq-list">
              <details class="faq-item" open>
                <summary>
                  <span>Do I need camera experience for the workshops?</span>
                  <span class="faq-icon">+</span>
                </summary>
                <div class="faq-ans">
                  No. You walk in at 9am having never touched a cinema camera and walk out at 5pm with a finished cut on your phone.
                </div>
              </details>
              <details class="faq-item">
                <summary>
                  <span>Why are there zero rounded corners in this system?</span>
                  <span class="faq-icon">+</span>
                </summary>
                <div class="faq-ans">
                  Rounded corners signal soft SaaS templates. The Manor is squared, industrial, and warehouse-grade across every medium.
                </div>
              </details>
              <details class="faq-item">
                <summary>
                  <span>Can I use two red highlight blocks in one H1?</span>
                  <span class="faq-icon">+</span>
                </summary>
                <div class="faq-ans">
                  Never. One key phrase per heading at most. If everything is highlighted in red, nothing is.
                </div>
              </details>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="rail rail-r" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    </div>

       08 · MOTION (v4.1) (#motion)

  <section class="sec-Band ground-ink-0" id="motion">
    <div class="frame">

            <span class="coord">0.15–0.18S EASE · -6PX UP-LEFT LIFT · 56–60S LINEAR MARQUEE · 0.25S STICKY BAR</span>
          </div>
          <h2 class="h2">Fast and snappy. <em>No bounce.</em></h2>
          <p class="lede" style="color:var(--ash);">
            Mechanical, abrupt, and disciplined. Never use elastic springs, cubic-bezier overshoot, or slow fade-ins. And when reduced motion is requested, everything stops.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:16px;margin-bottom:48px;">

                  <li><strong>Hairline rules</strong> instead of boxes; <strong>red square markers</strong> instead of bullets.</li>
                  <li><strong>Tables:</strong> red header row with bone mono text, hairline rows.</li>

<!-- ============================================================
     SELF-CONTAINED INTERACTIVE SCRIPT
============================================================ -->
<script>
(function () {
  /* Verbatim Markdown Document from Andrew */
  const RAW_MARKDOWN = `/* [Omitted to prevent infinite recursion] */`;

  const toastEl = document.getElementById('toast');
  let toastTimer = null;
  function showToast(msg) {
    if (!toastEl) return;
    toastEl.textContent = msg;
    toastEl.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    toastTimer = setTimeout(() => toastEl.classList.remove('show'), 2200);
  }

  function copyText(text, label) {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => showToast(label));
    } else {
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value = text;
      document.body.appendChild(ta);
      ta.select();
      document.execCommand('copy');
      document.body.removeChild(ta);
      showToast(label);
    }
  }

  function downloadMarkdown() {
    const blob = new Blob([RAW_MARKDOWN], { type: 'text/markdown;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'style-guide.md';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    showToast('DOWNLOADED STYLE-GUIDE.MD');
  }

  /* Copy / Download buttons */
  document.getElementById('copyPromptBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () =>
    copyText(RAW_MARKDOWN, 'COPIED FULL STYLE GUIDE (.MD)')
  );
  document.getElementById('copyMdNavBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () =>
    copyText(RAW_MARKDOWN, 'COPIED FULL STYLE GUIDE (.MD)')
  );
  document.getElementById('downloadPromptBtn')?.addEventListener('click', downloadMarkdown);
  document.getElementById('downloadMdNavBtn')?.addEventListener('click', downloadMarkdown);

  const cssStarter = document.getElementById('cssBoilerplateCode')?.innerText || '';
  document.getElementById('copyCssTokensBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () =>
    copyText(cssStarter, 'COPIED CSS :ROOT TOKENS')
  );
  document.getElementById('copyHtmlStarterBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () =>
    copyText(cssStarter, 'COPIED CSS BOILERPLATE')
  );

  /* Click-to-copy colour swatches */
  document.querySelectorAll('.swatch-card[data-copy]').forEach((card) => {
    card.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const hex = card.getAttribute('data-copy');
      copyText(hex, 'COPIED ' + hex);
    });
  });

  /* Texture placement toggle (Section vs Rails, from homepage-v4.1.html) */
  const texToggleBtn = document.getElementById('texToggleBtn');
  texToggleBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.body.classList.toggle('tex-inside');
    const inside = document.body.classList.contains('tex-inside');
    texToggleBtn.textContent = inside ? 'TEX: RAILS' : 'TEX: SECTIONS';
    showToast(inside ? 'DOT TEXTURE MOVED TO RAILS' : 'DOT TEXTURE ON SECTIONS (V4.1)');
  });

  /* Workshop Slab Horizontal Scroll Controls */
  const slabTrack = document.getElementById('slabTrack');
  document.getElementById('slabPrevBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    slabTrack?.scrollBy({ left: -320, behavior: 'smooth' });
  });
  document.getElementById('slabNextBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    slabTrack?.scrollBy({ left: 320, behavior: 'smooth' });
  });

  /* Sticky Service Bar Toggle */
  const stickyBar = document.getElementById('stickyBar');
  document.getElementById('toggleStickyBarBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    stickyBar?.classList.toggle('visible');
  });
  document.getElementById('closeStickyBarBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    stickyBar?.classList.remove('visible');
  });

  /* HyperFrames Mechanical Wipe Demo */
  const hfStage = document.getElementById('hfStage');
  const hfHeadline = document.getElementById('hfHeadline');
  const hfFrameCounter = document.getElementById('hfFrameCounter');
  const hfLines = [
    'TEXT SLAMS IN ON A <em>HARD CUT.</em>',
    'LEARN TO MAKE CONTENT. <em>FROM ZERO.</em>',
    'NO EXPERIENCE. NO GEAR. <em>NO AUDIENCE.</em>',
    'WE SHOOT IT. <em>YOU POST IT.</em>'
  ];
  let hfIdx = 0;
  document.getElementById('triggerHfWipeBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!hfStage) return;
    hfStage.classList.remove('wiping');
    void hfStage.offsetWidth;
    hfStage.classList.add('wiping');
    setTimeout(() => {
      hfIdx = (hfIdx + 1) % hfLines.length;
      if (hfHeadline) hfHeadline.innerHTML = hfLines[hfIdx];
      if (hfFrameCounter) {
        hfFrameCounter.textContent = 'FRAME 00' + ((hfIdx + 1) * 24) + ' // CUT 0' + (hfIdx + 1);
      }
    }, 200);
  });

  /* Video Grade Toggle (contrast 1.08, saturation 0.92) */
  const vidMonitor = document.getElementById('vidMonitor');
  const gradeStatusLabel = document.getElementById('gradeStatusLabel');
  document.getElementById('toggleGradeBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!vidMonitor) return;
    vidMonitor.classList.toggle('graded');
    const on = vidMonitor.classList.contains('graded');
    if (gradeStatusLabel) {
      gradeStatusLabel.textContent = on
        ? 'GRADE ACTIVE: CONTRAST(1.08) SATURATE(0.92)'
        : 'GRADE BYPASSED: RAW UNGRADED (1.00 / 1.00)';
    }
    showToast(on ? 'V4.1 GRADE APPLIED (1.08 / 0.92)' : 'GRADE BYPASSED');
  });

  /* Scroll Progress Bar & Active Section Spy */
  const progressBar = document.getElementById('progressBar');
  const trackedIds = [
    'scan', 'prompt', 'emphasis', 'colour', 'typography',
    'shape', 'shadows', 'layout', 'components', 'motion', 'formats', 'voice'
  ];
  const navPills = document.querySelectorAll('.sg-pill');
  const railLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.section-index a');

  function onScroll() {
    const scrollTop = window.scrollY || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
    const docHeight = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
    if (progressBar && docHeight > 0) {
      progressBar.style.width = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (scrollTop / docHeight) * 100)) + '%';
    }
    let currentId = 'scan';
    for (const id of trackedIds) {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el && el.getBoundingClientRect().top <= 160) {
        currentId = id;
      }
    }
    navPills.forEach((a) => {
      a.classList.toggle('active', a.getAttribute('href') === '#' + currentId);
    });
    railLinks.forEach((a) => {
      a.classList.toggle('active', a.getAttribute('href') === '#' + currentId);
    });
  }
  window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
  onScroll();
})();
</script>
</body>

PART 01 Foundations & Core Tokens (v4.1)
SECTIONS 01 – 06 · EMOTIONAL EMPHASIS · COLOUR · TYPOGRAPHY · TEXTURE · SHADOWS · LAYOUT
01 / Emotional Emphasis BRAND POSTURE · 15 CORE WORDS LOCKED · 5 FEEL CLUSTERS

Every piece should feel like this.

If it doesn't, it's off-brand, however tidy it looks.

CORE WORDS (LOCKED — ALL 15):
edgy squared (no round edges) bold cosmopolitan revolutionary raw urban unapologetic rebellious gritty underground punchy provocative confident brutalist
SUPPORTING WORDS, BY FEEL:
Edgy / rebellious
  • defiant
  • renegade
  • disruptive
  • maverick
  • irreverent
Raw / gritty
  • stripped-back
  • industrial
  • unpolished
  • rough-cut
  • honest
Bold / punchy
  • loud
  • striking
  • high-contrast
  • fearless
  • commanding
Urban / underground
  • street
  • warehouse
  • indie
  • DIY
  • grassroots
Straightforward
  • no-nonsense
  • blunt
  • direct
  • plain-spoken
  • no-frills
What It Is Not (Forbidden)
Never ship these traits.

Any trace of startup gloss, rounded corners, or decorative colour blends breaks the system immediately.

soft rounded pastel corporate friendly-startup glossy gradient-heavy "AI slop"
The 3-Point Quick Test
Run every layout through three blunt questions before shipping.
CHECK 01 // POSTURE
Would this look at home pasted on a warehouse wall?
CHECK 02 // GEOMETRY
Any rounded corners? (Must be zero everywhere.)
CHECK 03 // DISCIPLINE
Is there one clear red moment, or is red everywhere?
02 / Colour (v4.1) 13 LOCKED CSS TOKENS · 5 ALLOWED GROUNDS · CLICK ANY SWATCH TO COPY HEX

Ink, bone & signal red.

Five grounds only: ink, ink-0, bone, red, oxblood. Alternate dark and light sections for rhythm. Red is the action colour. No decorative gradients.

01. Five Grounds Only

Only ink, ink-0, bone, red, and oxblood are valid section backgrounds.

02. Section Rhythm

Alternate dark and light sections down the page so every boundary cuts sharply.

03. Red Is Action

Reserve signal red for buttons, one highlight block per heading, and 8px square markers.

04. Zero Gradients

No decorative gradients. The only permitted gradients are dark legibility overlays on photos.

03 / Typography (v4.1) ARCHIVO (VARIABLE WIDTH 125 · 112 · 100) + JETBRAINS MONO 700

Expanded heavy meets mono.

Archivo variable width handles display (125), lede (112), and body (100). JetBrains Mono 700 uppercase drives every eyebrow, coordinate, and label.

04 / Shape, Lines and Texture (v4.1) 0PX RADIUS · 1PX HAIRLINES · 315° HATCHED RAILS · DOT GRIDS

Zero radius. Everywhere.

No exceptions. Structural hairlines, 315° diagonal hatch rails, and mechanical dot grids create industrial depth without a single gradient or blur.

RADIUS: 0PX
NO EXCEPTIONS
RULE 01 // GEOMETRY
Zero Border Radius Everywhere

Enforced globally via * { border-radius: 0 !important; }. Buttons, inputs, video frames, badges, and tooltips are 90° sharp.

--line #3A3634
--bone-line #CFC7BB
RULE 02 // STRUCTURAL LINES
1px Hairlines (Dark & Light)

Use 1px solid var(--line) (#3A3634) on dark grounds and 1px solid var(--bone-line) (#CFC7BB) on light grounds.

315° · 1PX EVERY 10PX
RULE 03 // RAILS & PLACEHOLDERS
Hatched Rails & Diagonal Fill

repeating-linear-gradient(315deg, ... 0 1px, transparent 1px 10px) runs down both 40px side rails and fills empty placeholder frames.

12PX GRID · --INK
TEXTURE 01 // MAIN DARK
12px Dot Grid on Ink

1px bone dots at 8% opacity on a 12px × 12px grid across --ink sections and .slab-grid cards.

14PX GRID · OXBLOOD
TEXTURE 02 // OXBLOOD
14px Dot Grid on Oxblood

1px ink-0 dots at 28% opacity on a 14px × 14px grid across --red-deep oxblood sections.

24PX SPARSE · --INK-0
TEXTURE 03 // DEEPEST BLACK
24px Sparse Grid on Ink-0

1px bone dots at 5% opacity on a wide 24px × 24px grid for showreel and production sections.

05 / Hard Offset Shadows (v4.1) NO SOFT BLUR SHADOWS · SOLID OFFSETS ONLY · S1 THROUGH S5

Solid offsets only.

Zero blur radius on every shadow. Hierarchy is controlled strictly by border thickness, offset distance (10px, 8px, 4px, 14px), and shadow slab colour.

S1 · HERO
10PX RED

The single hero media item.

border: 2px solid var(--bone);
box-shadow: 10px 10px 0 var(--red);
S2 · LEAD MEDIA
8PX OXBLOOD

Lead images, showreel frames, portraits (ink border + ink shadow on bone).

border: 2px solid var(--bone);
box-shadow: 8px 8px 0 var(--red-deep);
S3 · TINTED PANEL
0PX FLAT

Tinted panel (--ink-2 or --bone-2), no shadow — groups of content.

background: var(--ink-2);
border: 1px solid var(--line);
S4 · SECONDARY
4PX DARK

Secondary frames, vertical vlog cards, thumbnails.

border: 1px solid var(--bone);
box-shadow: 4px 4px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35);
S5 · HOVER ME
-6PX LIFT → 14PX

Hover translate(-6px,-6px), shadow grows to 14px — cards and video frames.

transform: translate(-6px,-6px);
box-shadow: 14px 14px 0 var(--red);
S2 on Bone Ground Rule
ON BONE: 2PX INK BORDER + 8PX INK SHADOW

When an S2 frame sits on --bone, swap both the border and the 8px offset shadow to var(--ink) (#141312).

.ground-bone .s2 → 8px 8px 0 var(--ink)

07 / Components (v4.1) BUTTONS · TAGS · CARDS · ALBERS SQUARES · PLACEHOLDERS

Square slabs & hard controls.

Every component in homepage-v4.1.html is built from five core primitives: ground-flipping buttons, 1px bordered tags, solid colour slab cards, nested Albers squares, and honest mono placeholders.

COMPONENT 01 // BUTTONS (V4.1)
Square · Archivo 125 / 900 · 14px · 16 × 24px

Red, ink, bone, outline. Hover flips to the opposite ground in 0.15s ease. Use → for on-page links and ↗ for external links.

HOVER ANY BUTTON ABOVE TO VERIFY GROUND FLIP

Archivo 112 / 700 · Uppercase · 1px Border

Solid ground with 1px structural border. Used for metadata pills, formats, capacities, and status labels.

Three Nested Squares (0% / 20% / 40% Inset)

07.3 / 50:50 Split + Red Square Check List

Red squares replace bullets.

Never use default browser round disc bullets. Every list item sits between 1px hairlines and starts with a solid 8px or 10px --red square.

  • Dedicated industrial desk in Manor House N4
  • 10Gbps symmetrical fibre for raw footage uploads
  • Member credits toward studio and booth hire
  • No corporate beanbags. No startup ping-pong.
07.4 / Accordion Component (<details class="faq-item">)
Do I need camera experience for the workshops? +
No. You walk in at 9am having never touched a cinema camera and walk out at 5pm with a finished cut on your phone.
Why are there zero rounded corners in this system? +
Rounded corners signal soft SaaS templates. The Manor is squared, industrial, and warehouse-grade across every medium.
Can I use two red highlight blocks in one H1? +
Never. One key phrase per heading at most. If everything is highlighted in red, nothing is.
PART 03 Motion (v4.1), Derived Formats & Copy Voice
SECTIONS 08 – 10 · KINETICS · HTML TOOLS · PDFS · HYPERFRAMES · VIDEO & MOTION · COPY VOICE
08 / Motion (v4.1) 0.15–0.18S EASE · -6PX UP-LEFT LIFT · 56–60S LINEAR MARQUEE · 0.25S STICKY BAR

Fast and snappy. No bounce.

Mechanical, abrupt, and disciplined. Never use elastic springs, cubic-bezier overshoot, or slow fade-ins. And when reduced motion is requested, everything stops.

RULE 01 // HOVER
0.15–0.18s Ease

Colour and hover transitions snap in 0.15–0.18s ease. Zero bounce.

RULE 02 // LIFT
Up-Left 6px

Straight diagonal translate(-6px,-6px); shadow grows to 14px.

HOVER THIS CARD
RULE 03 // TICKER
56–60s Linear

Constant linear scroll, pauses immediately on hover.

SEE MARQUEE BELOW
RULE 04 // STICKY BAR
0.25s Slide-Up

Bottom sticky service bar slides up in 0.25s ease.

RULE 05 // A11Y
Reduced Motion

Under prefers-reduced-motion: reduce, everything stops.

0.001MS LOCKED

09 / Applying the System to Other Formats (Derived) HTML TOOLS · DOCUMENTS & PDFS · HYPERFRAMES ANIMATION · VIDEO & MOTION GRAPHICS

One system. Every medium.

Everything marked (derived) applies the v4.1 homepage rules to formats the homepage doesn't cover. Treat derived lines as a starting point, not a decision.

9.1 / HTML Pages and Tools (Derived)
Single Self-Contained File Starter
  • Single self-contained file.
  • Copy the Section 2 tokens as CSS variables.
  • Add *{border-radius:0 !important} globally.
  • Add the blue tap-highlight fix (-webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent).
:root {
  --ink: #141312;       --ink-0: #0D0C0B;     --ink-2: #1E1C1B;
  --line: #3A3634;      --bone: #ECE6DC;      --bone-2: #E7E0D4;
  --bone-line: #CFC7BB; --red: #D7261E;       --red-2: #B81C16;
  --red-deep: #8E1410;  --red-deeper: #6D100D;
  --ash: #9A938A;       --ink-muted: #4A453F;
}
*, *::before, *::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  border-radius: 0 !important;
}
html, a, button, summary, [role="button"] {
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}
a:focus-visible, button:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--red);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
9.2 / Documents and PDFs (Derived)
Live Cover & Interior Page Specimen
BONE PAGE GROUND · INK TEXT · RED/INK COVER · RED SQUARE MARKERS · MAX 1 RED HIGHLIGHT PER PAGE
THE MANOR · DOC-01 COVER / SECTION BREAK (RED OR INK)
WORKSHOP HANDBOOK.

Cover and section-break pages use solid --ink or --red ground with bone type.

MANOR HOUSE · N4 PAGE 01 // 12
SECTION 02 // CAMERA & LIGHTING RULES INTERIOR PAGE GROUND: --BONE (#ECE6DC)
ONE HIGHLIGHT BLOCK PER PAGE AT MOST.

Headings use Archivo 125 / 900 uppercase. Body uses Archivo 100. Labels and page numbers use JetBrains Mono. Use 1px hairline rules instead of boxes, and 8px red square markers instead of bullets:

  • Page ground: bone (#ECE6DC). Text: ink (#141312).
  • Hairline rules instead of boxes; red square markers instead of bullets.
  • Tables: red header row with bone mono text, hairline rows.

9.3 / HyperFrames / JS Animation (Derived)

Cuts, slides & straight wipes.

Hard, mechanical movement: no overshoot, no bounce, no blur-in. Short timings (0.15–0.3s) for UI motion; linear for tickers. Shapes enter as solid squares and bars.

HYPERFRAMES STAGE · 12PX DOT GRID FRAME 0024 // CUT 01
■ SOLID BAR ENTRY · 0.18S
TEXT SLAMS IN ON A HARD CUT.
TIMING: 0.15–0.3S · ZERO BLUR · ZERO BOUNCE
  • Same tokens as CSS variables. Same fonts (Archivo + JetBrains Mono).
  • Hard, mechanical movement: cuts, slides, straight-line wipes. No overshoot, no bounce, no blur-in.
  • Short timings (0.15–0.3s) for UI-style motion. Linear for continuous scrolls and tickers.
  • Shapes enter as solid squares and bars. Text slams in on a cut or wipes in behind a red block.
  • Backgrounds use hatch and dot-grid textures, not gradients.
9.4 / Video and Motion Graphics (Derived)

Lower-thirds, PiP & v4.1 grade.

Square-edged slabs for lower-thirds, S1/S2 hard offset shadows on picture-in-picture frames, hard cuts/wipes, and high-contrast desaturated grade (contrast 1.08, saturation 0.92).

REC ● 24FPS · 16:9 MASTER
■ CAM B (PiP) S2 OFFSET
0PX RADIUS + 8PX OXBLOOD SHADOW
WE SHOOT IT. YOU POST IT.
SPEAKER // STUDIO N4
ANDREW · THE MANOR
GRADE ACTIVE: CONTRAST(1.08) SATURATE(0.92)
  • Lower-thirds: mono label on a solid ink or red slab, square edges, red square marker.
  • Titles: Archivo 125 / 900 uppercase, red highlight block on the key phrase.
  • Frames & PiP: hard offset shadow (S1/S2 style), no rounded corners.
  • Transitions: hard cuts and straight wipes in red or ink. No dissolves, no glow.
  • Grade: contrast up, saturation slightly down (v4.1 hero image: contrast 1.08, saturation 0.92).
10 / Copy Voice SHORT · BLUNT · PLAIN WORDS · FULL STOPS DO THE WORK

Full stops do the work.

Short. Blunt. Plain words. Never invent facts, prices or client names. Use a plain placeholder until confirmed.

V4.1 CANONICAL LINE 01
"Learn to make content. From zero."
6 WORDS · 2 FULL STOPS
V4.1 CANONICAL LINE 02
"No experience. No gear. No audience."
6 WORDS · 3 FULL STOPS
V4.1 CANONICAL LINE 03
"We shoot it. You post it."
6 WORDS · 2 FULL STOPS
VOICE CONTRAST // ON-BRAND V4.1 VS OFF-BRAND SLOP
On-Brand (The Manor v4.1) Off-Brand (Forbidden)
"We shoot it. You post it." "Empowering creators with end-to-end storytelling solutions."
"Walk in and roll." "Seamlessly unlock your next studio production journey."
"Price placeholder — to confirm" Invented £499 pricing tiers or fake client testimonials.
HARD RULE // DATA INTEGRITY
NEVER INVENT FACTS, PRICES OR CLIENT NAMES.

Use a plain placeholder (e.g. Photo to come, Price placeholder, Client logo placeholder) until confirmed.
